We contribute an audit protocol requiring score basis and stack disclosure, blind probes, overwrite reporting, and rollout stability tests before using such scores for defensive driving claims.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient Online Lexicographic Generalized Low-Rank Matrix Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04324)
*Bo Xue,Ji Cheng,Haodong Jing,Hongzong Li,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究多优先级目标的广义低秩矩阵老虎机问题，提出Lexi-LowGLM算法，通过在线牛顿步更新目标特定估计器，将计算复杂度从O(T²)降至O(T)，并建立依赖有效低秩维度的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有广义低秩矩阵老虎机算法仅针对单一目标，且每次更新需重复求解批量广义线性估计器导致O(T²)计算开销；实际场景中常需同时处理多个具有优先级差异的目标，因此需要既能高效计算又能处理多目标优先级的学习算法。

Method: 算法分两阶段：首先估计各目标特定的低秩子空间，然后在降维特征空间中执行字典序学习。每个目标的估计器采用在线牛顿步更新而非批量全历史估计，从而避免重复计算。

Result: 对每个目标i得到遗憾界O~(W_i^lex√m(d1+d2)r√T)，依赖秩r和维度(d1+d2)的有效低秩量而非d1d2环境维度；在线更新使T轮估计器更新复杂度从O(T²)降为O(T)。数值实验验证了有效性和计算效率。

Conclusion: Lexi-LowGLM有效解决多优先级广义低秩矩阵老虎机问题，在保持统计效率的同时大幅降低计算复杂度，遗憾界随有效维度而非环境维度增长，适合高维多目标在线决策场景。

Abstract: This paper studies generalized low-rank matrix bandits with multiple prioritized objectives. At each round, the learner selects a matrix-valued arm and observes a vector-valued reward, whose components correspond to multiple objectives with different priority levels. Each objective is governed by an objective-specific generalized low-rank matrix model, and the learner evaluates arms according to a lexicographic preference order, prioritizing higher-level objectives before lower-level ones. We propose \textsc{Lexi-LowGLM}, an efficient online algorithm that first estimates objective-specific low-rank subspaces and then performs lexicographic learning in the reduced feature spaces. Unlike existing single-objective algorithms that repeatedly solve a batch generalized linear estimator using all historical observations, \textsc{Lexi-LowGLM} updates each objective-specific estimator via an online Newton step, reducing the estimator-update complexity over $T$ rounds from $O(T^2)$ to $O(T)$. We establish a regret bound of $\widetilde O\left(W_i^{\rm lex}\sqrt{m}\,(d_1+d_2)r\sqrt{T}\right)$ for each objective $i\in[m]$, where $r$ is an upper bound on the ranks of the objective-specific parameter matrices and $W_i^{\rm lex}$ characterizes the lexicographic trade-off effect. This bound depends on the effective low-rank dimension $(d_1+d_2)r$ rather than the ambient dimension $d_1d_2$. Numerical experiments further validate the effectiveness and computational efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [108] [Cost-Aware Multi-Objective Bandits: Theory and Application to Budgeted LLM Configuration Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04333)
*Bo Xue,Zhi Hong,Jiayi Li,Yuanyu Wan,Ji Cheng,Shuang Qiu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 将LLM配置评估建模为成本感知的多目标老虎机问题，提出两种算法：基于超体积的UCB用于在线配置选择，以及成本感知的经验间隙消除用于Pareto识别，并在有限预算下实现理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM配置评估面临预算有限、成本各异和多目标冲突的挑战，现有方法无法同时处理成本差异和多目标优化，因此需要一个新的理论框架来高效地平衡评估成本与多目标表现。

Method: 将评估过程形式化为成本感知的多目标老虎机问题；在线选择采用超体积UCB算法，优化每单位成本的乐观超体积指标；Pareto识别采用成本感知的间隙消除算法，结合配置成本与分类间隙定义复杂度。

Result: 在线选择获得预算遗憾上界O(sum log B / Δ_i)，保留对数预算依赖；Pareto识别错误概率上界为O(exp(-B/H_{μ,c}))，指数衰减并在成本相同时恢复标准保证；实验验证了框架在有限预算下的高效性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为LLM配置评估提供了理论严谨且实践有效的方法，支持在有限预算下进行在线决策和准确的成本感知Pareto识别，未来可扩展至更复杂的配置空间。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) configuration evaluation is challenging due to limited evaluation budgets, varying costs, and multiple competing objectives. In this paper, we formulate LLM configuration evaluation as a cost-aware multi-objective bandit problem, where each configuration evaluation incurs a configuration-dependent cost and yields a noisy vector-valued outcome. Under this framework, we study two fundamental problems: online configuration selection and Pareto configuration identification. For online configuration selection, we propose a hypervolume-based UCB algorithm that optimizes an optimistic hypervolume-per-cost index. We establish a budgeted regret bound of order $O\bigl(\sum_{i\ne i^\star}\frac{\log B}{Δ_i}\bigr)$, where $B$ is the evaluation budget, $i^\star$ is the optimal configuration in terms of hypervolume efficiency, and $Δ_i$ is the corresponding efficiency gap of configuration $i$. This bound retains the logarithmic budget dependence of classical single-objective budgeted bandits. For fixed-budget Pareto identification, we develop a cost-aware empirical gap elimination algorithm and prove that its error probability is of order $O\bigl(\exp(-\frac{B}{H_{μ,c}})\bigr)$, where $H_{μ,c}$ is a cost-aware Pareto identification complexity depending on configuration costs and Pareto classification gaps. This error probability decays exponentially with the evaluation budget and recovers the standard Pareto set identification guarantee when all configuration costs are identical. Experiments on LLM configuration evaluation tasks demonstrate that the proposed framework enables efficient online decision-making and accurate cost-aware Pareto identification under limited budgets.

</details>


### [109] [Non-asymptotic implicit bias of logistic regression at early-stage gradient descent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04382)
*Han Bao*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文从隐式偏置角度研究梯度下降在分类问题中的早期对齐现象，证明参数向量能在远少于渐近收敛所需迭代次数内与最大间隔方向弱对齐，并给出紧的迭代界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论仅证明最大间隔收敛是渐近行为，但其速率远慢于纯凸优化，而实践中参数在少量迭代内就已与最大间隔方向正相关。本文旨在解释这一早期阶段对齐现象的机制。

Method: 通过分解参数向量的径向与切向流动，直接在对齐动力学上结合数据集几何进行分析，避免了渐近展开，从而得到弱对齐的迭代复杂度上界。

Result: 证明参数向量在 O(exp(exp(-δ))) 次迭代内与最大间隔方向弱对齐（δ为允许误差），并证明该界是紧的，即无法在更少迭代内保证。

Conclusion: 该理论填补了早期对齐现象与渐近收敛之间的空白，表明即使在渐近速率很慢的情况下，弱对齐也能快速发生，为‘训练越久泛化越好’的实践提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Gradient descent has been of particular interest in modern machine learning beyond sole focus on optimization. Implicit bias emerging from optimization, though not being encoded by the learning objective, often prevents from overfitting to spurious patterns. A typical instance is the max-margin implicit bias of a linear classifier, widely established for exponentially tailed loss functions. Even after having a given dataset separated, the parameter vector continues to evolve towards the max-margin direction asymptotically along the gradient descent dynamics. This phenomenon corroborates a frequent empirical observation of "train longer, generalize better." However, the max-margin convergence is an asymptotic phenomenon, and what is worse, this asymptotic convergence rate is significantly slower than pure convex optimization. Even so, the parameter vector along gradient descent dynamics commonly correlates with the max-margin direction positively (though not exactly) within considerably fewer iterations than the asymptotic rate. By shedding another light on this classical problem, this work aims to understand the mechanism of this early-stage alignment phenomenon. Our theoretical results demonstrate that the parameter vector weakly aligns with the max-margin direction within $O(\exp(\exp(-δ)))$ iterations, where $δ>0$ is the permissible alignment error, which is shown to be tight. By tracking the radial and tangential flows, our proof operates on the alignment dynamics directly with dataset geometry and gets rid of the asymptotic expansion, which is a key insight to establishing faster weak alignment.

</details>


### [110] [Discretization and Statistical Consistency of Functional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04531)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出并证明了函数空间上流匹配算法的强L2收敛性，在非嵌套sigma代数条件下建立端到端Wasserstein界，并给出具体的数值验证与复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数流匹配方法虽在函数分布上定义，但实际实现基于有限系数或点值。在自适应或散乱细化下，条件sigma代数不嵌套，导致经典鞅收敛理论失效，需要新的收敛性证明。

Method: 对任意强一致有限秩重构序列证明有限条件速度目标的强L2收敛；引入正则空间处理点传感器扩展；通过非交换迹类高斯例子区分投影限制与精确条件；利用Bernstein论证和空间正则性-立方体认证器处理算子实现与超额风险项。

Result: 获得正交投影的定量界；全局Lipschitz激活下通过显式递推验证传感器无关常数；非交换例子给出边界乘子0与0.72的对比；获得固定模型维数下O~(n^{-1})的超额风险率及显式端到端速率（裁剪高斯缩放情形）。

Conclusion: 建立的收敛性理论不依赖嵌套sigma代数或有限维ODE唯一性，为自适应细化下函数流匹配提供了严格理论基础，并给出可验证的速率保证。

Abstract: Functional flow matching is posed on distributions of functions but implemented from finitely many coefficients or point values. Under scattered or adaptive refinement, the resulting conditioning sigma-algebras need not be nested, so martingale convergence does not justify the sensor limit. We prove strong $L^2$ convergence of finite conditional velocity targets for every strongly consistent sequence of finite-rank reconstructions, with quantitative bounds for orthogonal projections and a point-sensor extension through a regularity space. For learned flows, coupling directly to a population superposition path yields an end-to-end Wasserstein bound without assuming uniqueness of the population finite-dimensional ODE. We verify sensor-independent constants for a normalized quadrature neural operator, including globally Lipschitz activations through an explicit magnitude recurrence. A noncommuting trace-class Gaussian example gives boundary multiplier $0$ under projected restriction and $0.72$ under exact conditioning. A spatial regularity--cubature certificate closes the operator-realization term, a Bernstein argument gives a $\widetilde{O}(n^{-1})$ excess-risk term for fixed model dimension and envelopes, and an exactly realizable clipped Gaussian scaling specialization yields an explicit end-to-end rate.

</details>


### [111] [Rethinking Reservoir Pruning: A Dynamical Perspective for Echo State Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04593)
*Sudip Laudari,Puspa Raj Adhikari*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出动态模态剪枝（DMP），基于轨迹平均雅可比Gramian的主转移模态贡献度对储层神经元排序剪枝，仅重训读层，在混沌及真实时序基准上提升或保持预测精度并减少冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ESN剪枝多依赖静态连接或激活统计，忽视了对输入驱动状态转移起关键作用的神经元，导致冗余未被有效消除。

Method: 计算轨迹平均雅可比Gramian，提取主导转移模态，按神经元对模态的贡献度排序，剪除低影响单元，仅重训输出层。

Result: 在混沌和真实世界时间序列基准上，DMP在降低储层冗余的同时提升或保持了预测精度。

Conclusion: 动态影响力比静态结构重要性更适合作为储层细化的剪枝准则。

Abstract: Echo State Networks (ESNs) offer an efficient framework for temporal prediction, but their randomly initialized reservoirs are often over-parameterized and dynamically redundant. Existing pruning methods largely rely on static connectivity or activation statistics, which may overlook neurons that shape input-driven state transitions. We propose Dynamical Mode Pruning (DMP), a reservoir pruning method that ranks neurons by their contribution to dominant transition modes obtained from a trajectory-averaged Jacobian Gramian. DMP removes low-impact units and retrains only the readout. Experiments on chaotic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that DMP improves or preserves forecasting accuracy while reducing redundant reservoir components. Our results suggest that dynamical influence is a useful criterion for reservoir refinement beyond static structural importance alone.

</details>


### [112] [IMFACT: Counterfactual Explanations for Time Series via Intrinsic Mode Function Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04777)
*Udo Schlegel,Julian Rakuschek,Thomas Seidl,Andreas Holzinger,Tobias Schreck,Javier Del Ser*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出IMFACT框架，在经验模态分解空间中生成时间序列分类器的反事实解释，通过替换IMF分量实现类别翻转，优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡信号（如振动）的分类信息集中在特定频带，在原始特征空间扰动会破坏时间结构并产生物理上不合理的反事实样本。

Method: 将输入信号分解为固有模态函数（IMFs），按策略选择部分IMFs，用最近异类样本（NUN）的对应IMFs逐步替换，直至分类器输出目标类别；评估6种替换策略和多NUN循环扩展。

Result: 在FaultDetectionA和FruitFlies两个UCR基准上，基于方差的三NUN策略在可靠性和合理性指标上优于两种基线；三NUN循环在所有数据集上取得最佳邻近性。

Conclusion: IMFACT在分解空间中生成的反事实解释更物理可行且有效，方差导向的多NUN策略是较优选择。

Abstract: Oscillatory signals, such as vibration, carry class-discriminative information in specific frequency bands; perturbing them in raw feature space for counterfactual analysis easily destroys their temporal structure and produces physically implausible results. In this work, we introduce IMFACT (IMF-based counterfACTuals), a model-agnostic framework for generating plausible counterfactual explanations for time series classifiers that operates in the decomposition space of Empirical Mode Decomposition. An input signal is split into Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), and selected IMFs are progressively substituted with those of a Nearest Unlike Neighbour (NUN) until the classifier flips to the target class. We evaluate six IMF-selection strategies and a multi-NUN cycling extension on two UCR benchmarks (FaultDetectionA, FruitFlies). The variance-based strategy with three NUNs outperforms two prominent baseline techniques on reliability and plausibility metrics, while cycling across three NUNs yields the best proximity across both datasets.

</details>


### [113] [Above-ground Biomass Estimation with Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04792)
*Ghjulia Sialellia,Linus Scheibenreif,Jan Dirk Wegner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了地理空间基础模型（GFMs）在全球尺度地上生物量（AGB）估算中的表现，发现预计算嵌入产品（如AlphaEarth Foundations）显著优于作为冻结编码器使用的GFMs，甚至超越全监督SOTA模型，并展现出更强的时空泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GFMs在遥感领域兴起，但其在定量回归任务（如生物量估算）中的效用尚未被充分探索，现有基准多聚焦于分类和分割。全球尺度的AGB估算对碳监测至关重要，因此需要系统评估GFMs在此类回归任务中的实际性能。

Method: 利用AGBD数据集，通过PANGAEA框架评估11个GFMs（作为冻结编码器）及两个预计算嵌入产品（AEF和TESSERA）。对比全监督SOTA模型，测试地理与时间泛化能力，并与ESA CCI生物量产品进行一致性分析。

Result: 冻结编码器形式的GFMs显著劣于全监督SOTA；而AEF嵌入产品表现优异，MLP基于AEF嵌入即可超越SOTA，且将SOTA模型训练于AEF嵌入（可选加原始特征）取得最佳总体结果，同时时空泛化更优。

Conclusion: 预计算嵌入产品（如AEF）在全球AGB估算中更具实用价值，而传统冻结编码器GFMs在此回归任务中优势不足；建议未来优先采用嵌入增强策略以提升大尺度碳监测精度。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) from satellite imagery is essential for the large-scale monitoring of carbon stocks, yet it remains a challenging regression task at global scale. Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising machine learning paradigm to derive general-purpose representations from Earth observation data, but their utility for quantitative regression tasks like biomass estimation remains largely unexplored, as most benchmarks emphasize classification and segmentation. Here, we present a comprehensive benchmark of GFMs for global-scale AGB estimation using the AGBD dataset, a machine learning-ready benchmark spanning diverse biomes and geographies. We distinguish two ways in which GFMs reach practitioners: (i) models distributed as weights to be run by the user, which we evaluate as frozen encoders within the PANGAEA benchmarking framework; and (ii) models distributed as ready-to-use, pre-computed embedding products, for which we evaluate AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) and TESSERA. We compare 11 GFMs available on PANGAEA and both embedding products against a fully supervised state-of-the-art (SOTA) model, assess their geographical and temporal generalization abilities, as well as agreement with the ESA CCI biomass product on independent reference data. Our results show that GFMs run as frozen encoders substantially underperform with respect to the supervised SOTA model, whereas pre-computed embedding products prove highly effective. An MLP trained on AEF embeddings outperforms the supervised SOTA model trained on AGBD features, and the same SOTA model trained on AEF embeddings (optionally augmented with selected raw features) achieves the best overall result, while also generalizing better across space and time.

</details>


### [114] [Stochastic Emulation using Generalized Stratified Sampling for Performance-Based Risk Optimization of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05006)
*Isabela D. Rodrigues,Seymour M. J. Spence,Henrique M. Kroetz,André T. Beck*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出GSS-SPCE框架，通过分层采样与独立SPCE模拟器结合，改进极端响应尾部估计，在减少非线性评估次数的同时准确满足概率约束，用于结构PBRO优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPCE虽无需复制非线性分析，但在表征结构响应分布尾部极端行为时存在不足，影响可靠性分析精度。

Method: 采用广义分层抽样(GSS)按灾害强度划分输入空间，每层内训练独立SPCE模拟器，基于全概率定理重组各层条件超越概率以评估概率约束。

Result: 在两层钢框架屈曲约束支撑优化中，该方法准确估计响应分布（含尾部区域），并显著减少PBRO所需的非线性模型评估次数。

Conclusion: GSS-SPCE为结构PBRO提供了一种高效且精确的替代方案，兼顾尾部精度与计算效率。

Abstract: Metamodels are instrumental in reducing the computational burden associated with nested reliability analyses and optimization loops in Performance-Based Risk Optimization (PBRO) of structures under stochastic loads. In this context, stochastic emulators are particularly useful because they approximate response distributions while accounting for the intrinsic stochasticity of the simulator. Among these methods, Stochastic Polynomial Chaos Expansion (SPCE) is especially attractive because it does not require replications of nonlinear analyses at fixed input conditions. However, SPCE may present limitations in accurately representing extreme responses in the tails of structural response distributions. To address this limitation, this study proposes a framework that combines Generalized Stratified Sampling (GSS) with SPCE. The GSS scheme partitions the input space into strata according to the intensity of the hazard, improving the representation of extreme responses, while independent SPCE emulators are trained within each stratum. The conditional exceedance probabilities estimated in each stratum are then recombined using the total probability theorem to evaluate the probabilistic constraints. The proposed GSS-SPCE framework is applied to the optimal design of buckling-restrained brace cross-sectional areas in a two-story steel building. The objective is to minimize the initial construction cost while satisfying prescribed probabilistic performance constraints. Results show that the proposed framework accurately estimates structural response distributions, including their tail regions, while substantially reducing the number of nonlinear model evaluations required for PBRO.

</details>


### [115] [DASyR-LLM: Domain-Aware Symbolic Regression with LLMs for Kinetic Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05120)
*Roberto Aliaga Medina,Paulina Quintanilla,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出了一种将大语言模型嵌入符号回归的动力学模型发现框架，通过LLM的物化批判与新表达式提议，在四个仿真案例中将迭代次数减少41.7%-79.3%，且在超过半数运行中直接给出正确模型结构，同时保持预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归缺乏领域知识，常搜索出物理化学上不合理的动力学模型；需要一种机制将化学专业知识融入自动化模型发现过程，以减少实验迭代并提高模型可解释性。

Method: 构建迭代式SR框架，每轮由LLM执行两项任务：对当前最优候选模型进行定性物化批判，并基于SR生成模型与内置化学知识提议新的速率表达式；通过LLM模块与SR算法的协同搜索候选模型空间。

Result: 在多相催化与生物过程四个复杂度递增的案例中，与先进SR框架相比迭代减少41.7%-79.3%；LLM直接提出正确结构在超过一半引导运行中出现；验证集R²>0.98；消融表明SR与LLM规模均贡献性能，小型LLM仍保留大部分发现效率。

Conclusion: LLM能有效向科学模型发现注入领域知识，显著降低实验成本并保持预测准确性，为全自动、领域感知的动力学建模流程奠定基础。

Abstract: Kinetic model discovery is a central challenge in chemical engineering, as accurate rate expressions are essential for understanding and controlling chemical and biological processes. Symbolic regression (SR) has emerged as a powerful data-driven approach for identifying interpretable kinetic models, but usually operates without domain knowledge, often exploring physicochemically implausible models. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising avenue for injecting domain expertise into this search. Here, we introduce an LLM-guided SR framework, embedding an LLM module within an iterative SR algorithm for automated kinetic model discovery. The LLM performs two roles at each iteration: (1) a qualitative physicochemical critique of the best SR candidates, and (2) the proposal of new candidate rate expressions guided by the SR-generated models and embedded chemical knowledge. Our framework is evaluated on four in silico case studies of increasing complexity, spanning heterogeneous catalysis and bioprocess systems. Results show the LLM-guided framework reduces iterations to identify the ground-truth model by $41.7-79.3\%$ versus a state-of-the-art SR framework, with the LLM directly proposing the correct model structure in over half of the guided runs. In practical settings, where each iteration typically requires a new wet-lab experiment, this translates into a substantial reduction in experimental effort. Predictive performance on an independent validation set is equivalent between both approaches, with $R^2>0.98$ in all case studies. Ablation studies indicate that both the SR component and the LLM scale contribute to this performance, with a reduced-size LLM largely retaining discovery efficiency. These findings demonstrate that LLMs can effectively inject domain knowledge into scientific model discovery, paving the way toward fully automated, domain-aware kinetic modelling pipelines.

</details>


<div id='Sec'></div>

# Sec [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Securing Contrastive mmWave-based Human Activity Recognition against Adversarial Label Flipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04029)
*Amit Singha,Ziqian Bi,Tao Li,Yimin Chen,Yanchao Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 首次系统研究mmWave HAR在监督对比学习下的标签翻转投毒攻击，提出三种攻击及对应防御，并在原型系统上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mmWave HAR应用广泛但安全性未受重视，现有研究忽略标签投毒对对比学习的威胁，亟待填补空白。

Method: 设计三类标签翻转攻击策略，针对监督对比学习特性构建攻击模型，并开发相应防御机制；利用原型系统进行实验评估。

Result: 实验证明攻击能显著降低模型性能，防御措施可有效缓解攻击影响，且方法可扩展至其他无线HAR系统。

Conclusion: 强调无线HAR安全设计的重要性，攻击与防御方案具有普适性，为系统部署提供安全参考。

Abstract: Wireless Human Activity Recognition (HAR), leveraging their non-intrusive nature, has the potential to revolutionize various sectors, including healthcare, virtual reality, and surveillance. The advent of millimeter wave (mmWave) technology has significantly enhanced the capabilities of wireless HAR systems. This paper presents the first systematic study on the vulnerabilities of mmWave-based HAR to label flipping poisoning attacks in the context of supervised contrastive learning. We identify three label poisoning attacks on the contrastive mmWave-based HAR and propose corresponding countermeasures. The efficacy of the attacks and also our countermeasures are experimentally validated on a prototype system. The attacks and countermeasures can be easily extended to other wireless HAR systems, thereby promoting security considerations in system design and deployment.

</details>


### [117] [SoK: How Frontier AI Reshapes System-Level Security Risk Dynamics in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04033)
*Chandra Thapa,Mohan Baruwal Chhetri,Marthie Grobler,Shahroz Tariq,Tooba Aamir*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个五维风险动态框架，系统化分析前沿AI如何重构关键基础设施的安全风险，强调从模型中心鲁棒性转向生命周期结构的系统级保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全文献按攻击类型、生命周期阶段或资产类别组织风险，但未能捕捉FAI通过系统级动态（如风险如何产生、扩散和控制）重塑CI安全结果的方式。

Method: 引入五维风险动态框架：能力涌现、渗透路径、跨系统传播、控制权威退化、响应能力压力，并识别学术研究与CI运营约束间的结构性错配。

Result: 识别出重复出现的机制共同决定系统级风险，并推导出以部署为导向、基于系统级保障标准的研究议程。

Conclusion: 将注意力从模型中心鲁棒性转向生命周期结构的系统级保障，以应对互连CI环境中FAI带来的安全挑战。

Abstract: Frontier artificial intelligence (FAI), encompassing large-scale, general-purpose AI systems, including large language models, multimodal foundation models, and agentic systems, is increasingly integrated into critical infrastructure (CI). This challenges long-standing security assumptions of bounded behavior, segmented networks, component transparency, and human-paced decision-making. Existing AI-security literature typically organizes risks by attack type, lifecycle stage, or asset class, but fails to capture the system-level dynamics, such as how risk emerges, spreads, and is controlled, through which FAI reshapes CI security outcomes. This Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) introduces a five-dimensional risk-dynamics framework that characterizes how FAI reconfigures CI security across the lifecycle: (i) Capability Emergence through new FAI-enabled attack and defense capabilities, (ii) Infiltration Pathways through data, models and AI supply chains, (iii) Cross-System Propagation across interconnected infrastructures and dependencies, (iv) degradation of effective technical and human Control Authority, and (v) strain on institutional Response Capacity under operational pressure. Rather than enumerating threats, the framework identifies recurring mechanisms that jointly determine system-level risk. We further identify a structural mismatch between academic AI-security research and CI operational constraints, and derive a deployment-oriented research agenda grounded in system-level assurance criteria. Collectively, this work shifts attention from model-centric robustness to lifecycle-structured, system-level assurance in interconnected CI environments.

</details>


### [118] [A Multimodal Automatic Redteaming Evaluation based on Atomic Jailbreak Strategy Decoupling and Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04034)
*Shiji Zhao,Yuxuan Zhou,Chen Xiong,Dongxian Wu,Yang Bai,Xun Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种名为HACA的多模态自动红队越狱方法，通过分解文本-图像策略空间并采用跨模态联合规划器，实现对主流MLLMs平均95.48%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs越狱攻击缺乏原子化的多模态策略空间，且没有超越人工经验的简洁高效可执行框架。

Method: 将文本-图像越狱策略空间分解为结构、语义、句法三个层次，构建覆盖多模态的策略集；基于六维策略空间，使用跨模态联合规划器选择并组合原子越狱策略；在实现层面，使用统一生成执行器直接生成越狱指令。

Result: 在五个主流MLLMs上平均攻击成功率达95.48%。

Conclusion: HACA作为一种自动化红队方法，能系统性地组合多模态策略，高效暴露MLLMs的安全漏洞。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive progress in image-text comprehension and generation, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that can trigger harmful outputs and pose serious safety concerns. Existing multimodal jailbreak attacks have shown the feasibility of such attacks, but they still face two fundamental challenges: the lack of a atomic multi-modal strategy space, the absence of a concise and efficient executable framework beyond human-craft experience. To address these challenges, we first decompose the text-image jailbreak strategy space into three levels: structural, semantic, and syntactic, constructing a jailbreak strategy set encompassing both text and image modalities to systematically achieve combined coverage of different attack types. Then we propose a multimodal automated red team jailbreak method named Hierarchical Atomic Combination Attack (HACA). Specifically, based on a six-dimensional strategy space, a cross-modal joint planner is used to select and combine the different atomic jailbreak strategy for subsequent jailbreak command generation. Finally, at the implementation level, we explore to apply a unified generate executor to directly generate jailbreak instructions based on the selected multi-modal strategies. A series of experiments show that our automated red team method can achieve an attack success rate of average 95.48\% against five mainstream MLLMs.

</details>


### [119] [AMD SEV-SNP: A Confidential Computing Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04039)
*Amean Asad,Patrick McClurg,Patrick Woodhead*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文是AMD SEV-SNP硬件机密计算技术的技术入门，从硬件层面详细阐述了其如何通过内存加密、完整性校验和远程证明，构建对抗恶意hypervisor的可信执行环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟化环境中hypervisor拥有对客户机内存和寄存器状态的完全控制权，被视为信任基。SEV-SNP旨在改变这一信任模型，将hypervisor视为潜在敌手，通过硬件机制保护客户机数据机密性和完整性，并支持远程验证运行代码的真实性，满足云环境中对高安全隔离的需求。

Method: 基于AMD安全处理器和内存控制器中的加密引擎，实现客户机内存和寄存器状态加密（密钥hypervisor不可得）。利用反向映射表（RMT）提供内存完整性，对抗控制页表的攻击者。通过VM特权级别、加密的VMSA和GHCB协议实现客户机与不可信hypervisor的安全协作。最后，通过硬件签名的启动度量与AMD证书链绑定，构建远程证明流水线。

Result: 全面解释了SEV-SNP各核心安全保证的硬件实现路径，包括内存机密性、访问时篡改检测、以及与hypervisor安全通信的机制，并打通了从测量、签名到远程验证的完整证明链。

Conclusion: SEV-SNP通过硬件级设计将信任根从hypervisor转移到AMD安全处理器，使得在不可信云基础设施中运行高安全要求的虚拟机成为可能，为机密计算提供了一个从硬件原语到远程证明的完整技术方案。

Abstract: This paper is a technical primer on AMD Secure Encrypted Virtualization with Secure Nested Paging (SEV-SNP), a hardware confidential computing implementation that provides Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) for virtual machines. SEV-SNP treats the hypervisor as adversarial. It encrypts guest memory and register state with keys the hypervisor never possesses, detects any tampering with guest memory at the point of access, and lets a guest prove to a remote verifier exactly what code it is running. The paper constructs each of these guarantees from the hardware up. It opens with the threat model that drives the design and the hardware that enforces it, the AMD Secure Processor and the encryption engine in the memory controller. It then develops the mechanisms that make a confidential guest practical. The Reverse Map Table provides memory integrity against an adversary who controls the page tables. The privilege and communication machinery (VM Privilege Levels, the encrypted VM Save Area, and the GHCB protocol) lets the guest cooperate with a hypervisor it does not trust. The attestation pipeline binds a hardware-signed measurement of the guest's initial state to AMD's certificate chain, so a remote verifier can confirm independently what is running.

</details>


### [120] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出C2MOE框架，通过一致性与互补性引导的专家混合机制，统一表征学习与缺失模态填补，在多种缺失场景下超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖跨模态一致性学习但忽略互补性，导致缺失模态重建有偏，性能退化。

Method: 将多模态知识分解为一致性（最大化跨模态可预测性）与互补性（最大化条件熵）成分，构建双分支预测机制：一致性分支最小化不确定性以对齐联合分布，互补分支利用模态特有线索，并引入可学习重加权模块动态分配专家权重。

Result: 在多个MERC基准上，C2MOE在各种缺失模态设置下一致优于最先进方法，验证了鲁棒性与泛化性。

Conclusion: C2MOE有效兼顾一致性与互补性，为不完整多模态情感识别提供了鲁棒且自适应的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond the QBER Threshold: A Temporal QBER Based Machine Learning Framework for Multi Attack Detection in BB84 QKD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04047)
*Isha,Deepak Singh,Devesh Kumar,S. K Pal,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出基于时间QBER的机器学习框架，通过63个物理特征检测BB84 QKD中的窃听攻击，XGBoost达到88.01%准确率，将FNR从0.8477降至0.0198。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BB84 QKD依赖固定的11% QBER阈值检测窃听，但隐蔽攻击可低于该阈值而未被发现，需更精细的时序特征和机器学习方法提升安全性。

Method: 提取63个物理启发的时序特征（突发行为、时间不稳定性、基依赖不对称性、QBER损失交互），用随机森林、XGBoost和SVM-RBF分类器对七种攻击及正常信道进行识别，并用SHAP解释特征重要性。

Result: XGBoost最佳：准确率88.01%±0.47%，宏F1为0.8803；SVM-RBF表现相近。作为二分类检测器，固定阈值准确率仅25.82%且FNR为0.8477，而新框架FNR降至0.0198。

Conclusion: 时间QBER驱动的机器学习为BB84 QKD提供了准确、可解释且实用的多攻击安全监控方案，显著优于传统阈值方法。

Abstract: Conventional BB84 Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) systems rely on a fixed 11% Quantum Bit Error Rate (QBER) threshold to detect eavesdropping. However, stealthy attacks can remain below this threshold while still compromising channel security. This paper proposes a temporal QBER based machine learning framework for detecting and classifying eavesdropping attacks in BB84 QKD systems. Rather than relying on average session level QBER, the framework extracts 63 physics-informed temporal features capturing burst behavior, temporal instability, basis dependent asymmetry, and QBER loss interactions. Random Forest, XGBoost, and Support Vector Machine with a Radial Basis Function kernel (SVM-RBF) classifiers are evaluated on seven eavesdropping attacks and a normal channel scenario under noisy and lossy conditions. Averaged over ten independent runs, XGBoost achieves the best performance with 88.01% (0.47%) accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.8803, while SVM-RBF performs comparably, confirming the robustness of the proposed features. Evaluated as a binary attack-versus-normal detector for comparison with conventional monitoring, a fixed 11% QBER threshold achieves only 25.82% accuracy with a False Negative Rate (FNR) of 0.8477, whereas the proposed framework reduces the FNR to 0.0198, substantially improving detection of stealthy attacks that evade threshold-based monitoring. SHapley Additive exPlanations based (SHAP) explainability shows that physics-informed temporal and channel derived features are highly discriminative for identifying eavesdropping strategies. These results demonstrate that temporal QBER driven machine learning provides an accurate, explainable, and practical framework for multi attack security monitoring in BB84 QKD systems.

</details>


### [122] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文建立了次优势（极小极大）超度量算子在稀疏扰动下的ℓ0型稳定性理论，证明稀疏编辑仅通过最小生成树传播影响，并给出尖锐的每编辑暴露割分数和树全局包络，得到超度量条目变化数量的Hamming-Lipschitz界，同时证明了树几何依赖的不可避免性及条件近可加性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稳定性理论基于ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff度量，对于只改变少数成对距离的稀疏扰动过于宽松，无法刻画局部编辑的影响范围。需要一种ℓ0型理论来精确描述稀疏扰动下超度量值变化的条目数量。

Method: 通过分析最小生成树（MST）结构，将稀疏编辑的传播路径限定在树路径上。提出每编辑暴露割分数作为核心度量，构建树仅全局包络，推导Hamming-Lipschitz界；并通过严格割分离和显式构造族证明界的锐性，同时证明多编辑在认证大变化区域和可忽略重叠下的条件近可加性。

Result: 证明了超度量值变化仅发生在树路径跨越被编辑边或新暴露割的条目上；获得每编辑暴露割分数的锐界，树边界在严格割分离下精确达到，离树编辑可改变Θ(n²)个条目；多编辑条件下满足近可加性。实验验证了结构分数作为层次表示脆弱性诊断的有效性。

Conclusion: 该ℓ0稳定性理论为稀疏扰动下的层次聚类鲁棒性提供了精确刻画，确立了树几何作为传播核心的重要性，并为实际应用中的脆弱性分析提供了可计算的结构化诊断工具。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [123] [When Modalities Fail to Tango: Conformal Backdoor Detection in Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04052)
*Yiming Chen,Kemou Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CASCADE，一种基于共形预测的两阶段粗到细后门检测框架，通过统计置信边界和文本空间非一致性分数，在CC3M数据集上实现高检测率与低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIPScore的后门检测存在两个缺陷：良性与中毒样本的分数分布重叠大，固定阈值无法为模糊样本提供统计保证，导致检测可靠性不足。

Method: 利用共形预测量化不确定性，先通过跨模态一致性粗筛高置信样本，再在高置信中毒样本构建的参考集上计算文本空间相似度非一致性分数，以精确识别未定子集中的潜在中毒对。

Result: 在CC3M数据集上，针对多种攻击平均FPR为5.79%（TPR为100%），平均AUROC达0.9867，且对自适应攻击有效。

Conclusion: CASCADE通过共形预测提供了带统计保证的检测边界，显著提升多模态后门检测的可靠性和鲁棒性，优于现有依赖CLIPScore的固定阈值方法。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks in multimodal contrastive learning (MCL) have garnered growing attention in recent years, as many downstream tasks critically depend on pre-trained MCL models. Existing detection-based defenses predominantly rely on the CLIPScore metric, under the assumption that poisoned pairs exhibit lower semantic similarity between the image and the caption. However, we identify two critical flaws remaining in existing methods: (1) the substantial overlap between CLIPScore distributions of benign and poisoned pairs undermines the reliability of this metric, and (2) fixed-threshold detection cannot provide statistical guarantees for ambiguous samples within overlapping regions. To overcome these limitations, we propose integrating conformal prediction (CP), a statistical framework that quantifies uncertainty through nonconformity scores (NCSs), to establish provable confidence bounds for detecting poisoned image-caption pairs. Building on CP, we introduce CASCADE, a novel two-stage Coarse-to-Fine Conformal Backdoor Detection framework. The coarse-grained stage uses cross-modality consistency to identify high-confidence benign and poisoned pairs. In the fine-grained stage, a reference set is constructed from high-confidence poisoned pairs, and instance-level NCSs based on text-space similarity are computed for each sample in the unidentified subset. These NCSs measure conformity to the poisoning distribution and enable precise identification of latent poisoned pairs within the unidentified subset. Extensive experiments on the large-scale CC3M dataset demonstrate that CASCADE achieves an average FPR of 5.79% at 100% TPR and an average AUROC of 0.9867 across diverse attacks, while remaining effective against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [124] [AgentAntibody: An Adaptive Immune System for Defending LLM Agents against Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04053)
*Shihao Weng,Yang Feng,Xiaofei Xie,Jiongchi Yu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出AgentAntibody，一种受适应性免疫启发的LLM智能体防御框架，通过持久抗体库从历史任务中学习用户安全边界并动态进化，以抵御提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御将每次任务视为独立问题，忽略了用户请求的欠指定性——攻击可利用模糊性使智能体以用户拒绝的方式完成任务；用户期望随具体案例逐步清晰，防御应从每次交互中学习并迁移到后续任务。

Method: AgentAntibody将用户安全边界的演化理解编码为持久抗体库；运行时，该库识别对边界的威胁并触发相应免疫响应；随多次遭遇，抗体库进化以强化对未来攻击的免疫力。

Result: 在三个基准和四种骨干LLM上的广泛实验表明，通过经验学习用户边界，AgentAntibody在阻止有害行为的同时保留合法任务完成方面优于现有防御，即使有害与合法行为都兼容所述任务。

Conclusion: 基于经验演化抗体库的防御能有效应对提示注入，尤其在任务陈述模糊时平衡安全性与任务完成度，为LLM智能体的自进化安全机制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Prompt injection remains a critical threat to LLM agents, yet existing defenses treat each task as a self-contained problem, independent of previous encounters. In practice, user requests are often underspecified: they describe the desired outcome without fully specifying acceptable behavior. An injection can exploit this ambiguity, causing the agent to complete the task in a way the user would reject. As the user's expectations become clearer through concrete cases, a defense should learn from each encounter and apply what it learns to the next. Inspired by adaptive immunity, we propose AgentAntibody, which equips LLM agents with a self-evolving immune system against prompt injection. AgentAntibody represents its evolving understanding of the user's security boundary as a persistent library of antibodies. At runtime, the library recognizes threats to this boundary and mounts corresponding immune responses. Across encounters, it evolves to strengthen the agent's immunity to future attacks. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks and four backbone LLMs show that, by learning the user's boundary through experience, AgentAntibody outperforms existing defenses in preventing harmful actions while preserving legitimate task completion, even when the harmful and legitimate actions are both compatible with the stated task.

</details>


### [125] [An Inline Control Architecture for Language Models in Intelligent Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04065)
*Narendra Kumar Dewangan,Mounira Msahli*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Guard-V2X，一种面向V2X场景中LLM服务的在线语义护栏架构，通过多层防护在满足实时约束的同时显著降低对抗性提示入侵成功率，并消除不安全输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X系统引入LLM处理语义任务，但现有安全机制仅关注认证与消息完整性，无法应对提示层面的攻击，缺乏针对实时约束下的LLM服务防护方案。

Method: 设计内联护栏架构，包含规则化入口过滤、轻量安全分类器、策略约束的生成、可信检索及事后裁决，并采用四阶段实验流程（入侵分析、标定与延迟基准、护栏验证、对抗压力）进行评估。

Result: 在多轮对抗测试中，未防护及仅提示防护的基线仍存在漏洞，而Guard-V2X持续降低入侵接受率，并在两轮设置中消除不安全完成，同时未超出V2X语义咨询路径的延迟预算。

Conclusion: Guard-V2X能在实时约束下为LLM赋能的V2X服务提供可验证的安全边界，弥补传统机制不足，但其效果依赖于多层协同，两轮外鲁棒性需进一步研究。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-everything (V2X) systems increasingly incorporate large language models (LLMs) for semantic tasks such as message summarization, operator assistance, and decision support at roadside units and edge nodes. Although these components are not part of safety-critical control loops, they introduce prompt-level attack surfaces that are not addressed by traditional V2X security mechanisms focused on authentication and message integrity. This paper presents Guarded-V2X, an inline semantic guardrail architecture for securing LLM-enabled V2X services under real-time constraints. The proposed system integrates rule-based ingress filtering, a lightweight safety classifier, policy-constrained structured generation, trusted-only retrieval, and post-decision adjudication to enforce machine-checkable safety boundaries prior to downstream execution. Guarded-V2X is evaluated using a four-stage experimental pipeline encompassing intrusion vulnerability analysis, calibration and latency benchmarking, guardrail validation, and robustness under adversarial stress. Experiments are conducted on a V2X-aligned simulated dataset derived from RSU advisories, operator messages, and annotated V2X message summaries. Results show that unguarded and prompt-only baselines retain residual vulnerability under multi-turn adversarial trials, while Guarded-V2X consistently reduces intrusion acceptance success rates and eliminates observed unsafe completions in two-turn settings, without exceeding latency budgets for V2X semantic advisory paths.

</details>


### [126] [FBID: Adaptive Personalized Federated Learning for Robust Out-of-Distribution Attack Detection in IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04073)
*An Khanh Bui,Cong Thanh Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Pham,Hoang Thai Dinh,Diep N. Nguyen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出FBID框架，通过服务器端多臂老虎机动态调控客户端本地训练强度，并结合信任融合机制平衡全局与局部模型，显著提升异构IoT环境下非独立同分布数据的OOD攻击检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法依赖客户端自我调整，在高度非IID数据下易过度个性化，导致OOD攻击检测性能大幅下降。

Method: FBID在服务器端使用上下文多臂老虎机根据客户端行为与更新质量动态调节其本地训练强度，并引入基于信任的混合机制计算客户端专属的全局-局部模型插值系数。

Result: 在CICIoT2023数据集上，相比最强稳定基线，单个客户端OOD检测率相对提升最高7.66%，F1分数相对提升最高5.08%，且对未知攻击类别鲁棒性增强。

Conclusion: 服务器端个性化控制能有效缓解过度个性化问题，在保留全局攻击知识的同时实现有益局部适应，提升OOD检测鲁棒性。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) has emerged as a promising solution for intrusion detection in heterogeneous IoT environments, as it can improve local adaptation under highly Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (non-IID) data distributions. However, existing PFL methods often rely on client-side self-adjustment, which may lead to over-personalization and substantial degradation in out-of-distribution (OOD) attack detection. In this paper, we propose Federated Bandit Intrusion Detection (FBID), a novel adaptive PFL framework to address this limitation through server-side personalization control. In particular, FBID employs a contextual multi-armed bandit at the server to dynamically regulate each client's local training intensity according to its observed behavior and update quality. Moreover, FBID introduces a trust-based blending mechanism to derive client-specific interpolation coefficients between the global and local models, thereby preserving global attack-detection knowledge while still allowing beneficial local specialization. Through extensive experiments on the CICIoT2023 dataset under heterogeneous client distributions and OOD stress-test settings, we show that FBID improves individual client OOD Detection Rate (DR) by up to 7.66% and F1-Score (F1) by up to 5.08% (relative) over the strongest stable baseline, while also improving robustness to previously unseen attack classes.

</details>


### [127] [NEBULA: A Language - Independent Specification for Opaque Rotating Refresh Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04115)
*Matteo Teodori*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出NEBULA规范，为RFC 9700刷新令牌模型提供精确、语言无关的规范及10种参考实现，通过行为套件数据化消除转录偏差，并设计compare-and-set旋转契约以关闭并发重用绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RFC 9700仅规定策略未规定机制，导致各实现在线格式、存储、验证顺序、并发及边界情况上存在分歧，直接影响安全结果；需要精确的规范以减少实现分叉。

Method: 定义不透明令牌结构（128位选择器+256位验证器），将有效性归于服务端状态；发布38个场景的机器可读行为套件，各语言通过薄运行器执行；引入compare-and-set旋转契约处理并发刷新。

Result: 提供10种语言的一致实现，说明并发下重用检测绕过被关闭，分析包括后量子在内的安全属性，报告跨语言一致性方法有效性。

Conclusion: NEBULA展示了用数据驱动行为套件和精确契约实现多语言安全规范的方法，已开源，为RFC 9700落地提供可验证的参考。

Abstract: Refresh tokens are among the most sensitive credentials in modern authentication systems: long-lived, bearer-style, and sufficient to mint access tokens for days or weeks. RFC 9700, the current Best Current Practice for OAuth 2.0 security, mandates that refresh tokens issued to public clients be rotated on every use with replay (reuse) detection, or be sender-constrained. But the BCP specifies policy, not mechanism: it prescribes no wire format, no storage schema, no ordering of verification steps, no concurrency contract, and no semantics for edge cases such as lost-response retries or key rotation. Implementations may therefore diverge in precisely the corner cases that determine security outcomes.
  We present NEBULA, a precise, language-independent specification of the RFC 9700 refresh-token model, together with ten conformant reference implementations (TypeScript, Python, Go, Rust, Java, PHP, C#, Ruby, Elixir, Dart). NEBULA tokens are opaque -- a 128-bit public selector and a 256-bit secret verifier, both CSPRNG output, carrying no claims and no signature -- so token validity is a property of server-side state rather than of cryptographic verification. Its conformance methodology publishes the behavioural suite as data rather than as prose: 38 scenarios in one machine-readable file that every implementation executes through a thin per-language runner, so that drift by transcription is structurally excluded. We describe the specification -- including a compare-and-set rotation contract that closes a reproducible bypass of reuse detection under concurrent refresh -- analyse its security properties including its post-quantum posture, and report on cross-language conformance as a method for multi-implementation security specifications. The specification, implementations, and conformance artefacts are open source under the Apache License 2.0.

</details>


### [128] [Adversarially Robust Abductive Fusion of Pre-trained Transformer-based Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04190)
*Mario Leiva,Yue Ma,Qinru Qiu,Gerardo Simari,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种无需领域知识的元认知层，利用模型训练嵌入的向量几何构建标签向量池，通过整数规划求解检测器融合，在分布偏移和协同攻击下优于多数投票基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的预训练模型在分布偏移下性能下降，现有组合方法（如多数投票）在召回率和精度间权衡且对协同失败脆弱，而先前元认知方法依赖手工领域知识，无法泛化到新型场景。

Method: 构建每个模型自身的标签向量池，利用检测结果相对训练原型的几何关系生成错误检测规则，保持神经符号框架；将多ViT检测器融合建模为一致性溯因问题，测试时通过精确整数规划或多项式启发式求解。

Result: 在15个天气偏移测试集和6个ViT检测器上，无领域知识方法在干净数据上匹配最强多数投票变体（F1差0.005），在90%标签翻转攻击下平均F1达0.42，相对MV-Plurality提高22%，翻转率超过0.4时在所有测试集上取得最高F1。

Conclusion: 基于向量几何的元认知层可替代手工领域知识，实现同等错误检测性能，并在协同攻击下保持鲁棒性，且保留神经符号框架的可扩展性以融合额外知识。

Abstract: Deploying pre-trained perception models in novel environments degrades their accuracy under distributional shift, and assembling them alone does not recover it: combiners such as majority voting trade recall for precision and are brittle to coordinated failures. Prior metacognitive methods learn logical rules that flag a model's errors, but rely on hand-authored domain-knowledge cues (object-size priors, segmentation masks) that do not transfer to genuinely novel scenes. We show that this metacognitive layer can be learned without any domain knowledge by exploiting vector-space geometry: per-model Label Vector Pools (LVP), built from each model's own training embeddings, yield error-detection rules from the geometry of detections relative to training-determined prototypes, reaching parity with domain-knowledge rules to within $0.002$ every F1 on test set. Because the approach remains neurosymbolic, these geometric rules share a single logical framework and can still be complemented by domain knowledge when available. We frame the fusion of multiple imperfect ViT-based detectors as a consistency-based abduction problem solved at test time by an exact Integer Program (IP) and a polynomial-time heuristic. On an aerial-imagery benchmark of 15 weather-shifted test sets and six ViT detectors, our domain-knowledge-free layer matches the strongest majority-vote variant on clean data (within $0.005$ F1) and, unlike every majority-vote baseline, retains its performance under a coordinated label-flipping attack: at a $90\%$ flip rate it averages $0.42$ F1 versus $0.35$ for MV-Plurality (a $22\%$ relative gain) and attains the highest F1 on \emph{every} test set once the flip rate exceeds $0.4$

</details>


### [129] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文针对航空发动机剩余寿命预测的联邦学习场景，系统评估了良性异构（不同运行条件）和对抗异构（恶意投毒）的挑战，发现共享表征个性化可缩小70%的本地与集中式误差差距，而物理启发的传感器值后门攻击在标准聚合下成功率94.9%且不降低干净精度，Krum聚合是唯一能抵御协同攻击的聚合器，个性化与鲁棒聚合结合可恢复安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许航空机队在不共享原始遥测数据的前提下联合训练剩余寿命预测模型，但面临两类异构问题：诚实客户端因运行条件不同产生的良性数据分布差异，以及被攻陷客户端提交恶意更新的对抗性差异。现有研究少有将两者结合，且常规精度指标可能掩盖安全漏洞，因此需在安全导向的受控实验中评估不同解决方案。

Method: 使用多任务一维卷积神经网络，在C-MAPSS基准上构造结构非独立同分布划分，对比四种良性异构修复方法（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务端重加权等），并对四种聚合方法（标准平均、Krum等）实施五种攻击，包括一种基于物理传感器值、用于掩盖发动机退化模式的后门攻击。

Result: 共享表征个性化使本地与集中式均方根误差差距缩小约70%，远优于近端正则化（21%）和服务端重加权（10%）。物理后门攻击在标准平均聚合下成功率94.9%，且干净精度无统计显著变化；Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级并能抵御协同攻击，而个性化单独无防御作用。将个性化与鲁棒聚合结合后攻击成功率降至2.8%，仅引入微小精度损失。结果在不同客户端数量和更难的六条件数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 精度指标无法单独证明模型安全性，必须显式评估攻击成功率；Krum是唯一稳健的聚合器，但个性化与鲁棒聚合之间存在权衡：鲁棒更新选择会削弱协作表征学习。建议结合个性化与鲁棒聚合以兼顾准确性和安全性，并公开代码与数据划分以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [130] [Open-World Darknet Traffic Recognition Under Leave-One-Service-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04167)
*Javeriah Saleem,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开放世界暗网流量分类框架，采用留一服务评估和基于不确定性的分类方法，揭示从封闭世界到开放世界设置下性能显著下降，且行为重叠是可靠分类的主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗网流量识别研究大多基于封闭世界评估，假设所有服务类别在训练和测试中已知，这与现实环境不符，导致部署鲁棒性被高估。

Method: 采用留一服务评估模拟开放世界场景，结合随机森林和XGBoost模型，并引入基于不确定性的拒绝机制以处理未知服务类别。

Result: XGBoost在I2P环境中的Macro-F1从88.8%降至46.1%，随机森林从87.4%降至45.7%；不确定性拒绝仅略微提升鲁棒性，且语义吸收分析显示FreeNet视频流量被88.1%归类为浏览流量，I2P点对点流量被83.4%归类为FTP相关行为。

Conclusion: 封闭世界评估显著高估模型部署性能，行为重叠是开放世界暗网流量分类的核心难题，需进一步研究以提升实际场景中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Darknet traffic recognition is critical for cyber threat intelligence, as anonymity networks are often used to conceal malicious activity. However, most existing studies rely on closed-world evaluation, assuming all service categories are known during training and testing, which is unrealistic in real-world environments. This paper presents an open-world darknet traffic classification framework using leave-one-service-out evaluation and uncertainty-aware classification with Random Forest and XGBoost models. Experimental results demonstrate significant performance degradation when transitioning from closed-world to open-world settings, demonstrating that closed-world evaluation substantially overestimates deployment robustness. For example, XGBoost Macro-F1 decreases from 88.8% to 46.1% in the I2P environment, while Random Forest performance drops from 87.4% to 45.7%. Although uncertainty-based rejection slightly improves robustness, strong behavioral similarity between known and unknown services leads to frequent misclassification. Semantic absorption analysis further shows that FreeNet video traffic is classified as browsing traffic with an 88.1% assignment rate, while I2P peer-to-peer traffic is absorbed into FTP-related behavior with an 83.4% assignment rate. The findings demonstrate that behavioral overlap remains a major challenge for reliable open-world darknet traffic classification.

</details>


### [131] [Behavioral Skill Reconstruction: Reconstructing Hidden Functionality from LLM Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04192)
*Peichun Hua,Haoxuan Xu,Mengyuan Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出行为技能重建（BSR）攻击概念及SkillClone方法，证明仅通过合法交互即可克隆隐藏的闭源技能功能，文件保密无法保证功能保密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御仅针对提示注入导致的文件泄露，但即使文件不泄露，攻击者仍可能通过观察输入输出行为重建技能功能，这构成根本性安全威胁。

Method: SkillClone采用黑盒攻击流程：从公开广告推断接口假设，构造良性探针查询，合成可执行副本，并通过差分验证迭代修复以逐步逼近受害者技能行为。

Result: 在30个技能（规则、表格、流程、算法）上，SkillClone对多个目标的保留输入实现精确或部分恢复；单轮重建遗漏可通过迭代查询弥补，且现有披露防御和模糊描述均无法有效阻止。

Conclusion: 文件保密不等于功能保密；防御需同时限制累积行为信息泄露，而非仅阻止文件访问。

Abstract: Closed source agent skills may encode proprietary instructions, scripts, constants, and data. Providers may offer their capabilities as services while keeping the underlying packages hidden. Prior work focuses on prompt injection attacks that directly disclose these artifacts, and existing defenses accordingly aim to prevent such leakage. However, preventing file disclosure does not prevent users from recovering the functionality those files implement. This raises a fundamental question: can a user reconstruct a skill's functionality through ordinary use while its files remain hidden?
  We study behavioral skill reconstruction (BSR), in which an attacker uses valid task requests and observed responses to build a functional clone of a hidden skill. We introduce SkillClone, a black-box attack that clones a target skill by forming an interface hypothesis from its public advertisement, issuing structured benign probes, synthesizing an executable replica, and iteratively repairing it through differential validation against the victim skill. Across 30 skills spanning rules, tables, procedures, and algorithms, SkillClone achieves exact or partial recovery on held-out inputs for several targets. Iterative requerying closes gaps missed by single-round reconstruction. Because SkillClone uses only legitimate interactions, disclosure-focused defenses provide limited coverage, and less detailed skill descriptions offer limited protection. These results show that file secrecy alone does not ensure functional secrecy. Defenses must also limit cumulative information leakage from ordinary use.

</details>


### [132] [SafeCommit: Certifying When Memory-Grounded Agents May Safely Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04289)
*Mayur Akewar,Ravi Ranjan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeCommit，一种在智能体推理与外部执行之间加入的风险控制层，通过构建校准的潜在世界集合，仅在行动被所有保留世界认证为安全时才允许执行，从而解决长期智能体在内存不确定性下的过早承诺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期智能体依赖持久内存和工具执行具有外部副作用的行动，但内存可能过时、冲突、不完整或损坏，导致过早承诺这一核心失败模式。现有方法缺乏对行动时机的风险控制，未能在证据不足时阻止不安全行动。

Method: SafeCommit层利用内存、观察、工具输出、来源和策略约束构建校准的潜在世界集合；采用共形预测保证世界覆盖概率；对候选动作生成共形行动证书，仅当在所有保留世界中安全时放行；否则选择低副作用探针或保守回退；风险界将校准误差与表示误差分离。

Result: 在无依赖的受控模拟器中，该框架清晰展示了安全性与效用之间的权衡，并能一键复现所有报告结果；在满足校准世界覆盖的条件下，不安全认证提交的概率不超过目标水平α。

Conclusion: SafeCommit提供了一种具体方法，不仅决定智能体应做什么，还决定何时现有证据足以安全地执行，为长期智能体的安全决策提供了可验证的风险控制机制。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly use persistent memory and tools to take actions with external side effects. A central failure mode is premature commitment: an agent acts before resolving whether its memory grounding is stale, conflicting, incomplete, or corrupted. We formalize this problem as safe commitment under memory uncertainty and introduce SafeCommit, a risk controlled layer between agent reasoning and external execution. The layer constructs a calibrated set of plausible latent worlds from memory, observations, tool outputs, provenance, and policy constraints. It permits a side effectful action only when a conformal action certificate shows that the action is safe in every retained world. Otherwise, it selects a low-side-effect probe that targets the worlds blocking certification, or returns a conservative fallback. Under calibrated world coverage, the probability of an unsafe certified commit is at most the target level α; with imperfect world proposal, the bound separates calibration and representation error. A dependency-free controlled simulator illustrates the safety-utility tradeoff and reproduces all reported results with one command. The goal is to offer a concrete approach for deciding not only what an agent should do, but when the available evidence is sufficient to safely do it.

</details>


### [133] [Adversarial Attacks for Good: A Survey of Proactive Protection across the Visual Content Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04314)
*Jiaming Zhang,Boyang Chen,Zherui Li,Fuyao Zhang,Xinyu Yan,Hong Xi Tae,Wenwen He,Xuan Wang,Siqi Guo,Junhao Dong,Kun Wang,Hanxun Huang,Yige Li,Xingjun Ma,Yang Cao,Lingjuan Lyu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本综述探讨了‘用于善意的对抗攻击’这一保护范式，涵盖视觉内容生命周期中五个独立发展的研究社区，并沿共享轴评估其可迁移性、适应性和部署就绪度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉内容进入AI管道后，所有者对其使用方式缺乏技术控制，法律手段不足，因此需要在内容发布或访问时提前施加技术干预，以抵制未经授权的自动化处理或支持事后问责。

Method: 将五个研究社区（隐私过滤、不可学习示例、生成式防护、对抗性CAPTCHA、溯源机制）统一在‘对抗攻击用于善’框架下，比较其利用人机感知差异的共同原理，并从可迁移性、适应性和部署就绪度三个维度进行交叉评估。

Result: 大多数保护方法仅在静态或弱自适应对抗者下得到验证，缺乏超越受控基准的真实世界证据，且各社区间成功标准不兼容，难以直接比较。

Conclusion: 该范式在多模态模型与自主代理时代仍具相关性，但需整合跨阶段对策，解决鲁棒性、可组合性与可部署性等开放问题，以实现有效的所有者侧保护。

Abstract: Once visual content enters an AI pipeline, its owner often retains little technical control over how it is used. Legal and regulatory remedies can address misuse, but many technical interventions must be applied earlier, when content is released or accessed. This survey examines the protective paradigm that has grown around this intervention point, which we call \emph{adversarial attacks for good}. Perturbations and structured signals long studied as attacks on learned models are instead applied by data owners, creators, platforms, or auditors to disrupt unauthorized automation or support later accountability. Five research communities have arrived at this inversion largely independently, each addressing a different stage of a visual asset's lifecycle: privacy filters against unwanted recognition at sharing time, unlearnable examples against unauthorized training, generative safeguards against malicious editing or imitation, adversarial CAPTCHAs for access control against automated agents, and provenance mechanisms for post-circulation attribution. Although developed in separate venues with incompatible success criteria, many of these methods exploit persistent gaps between human perception, semantic interpretation, and machine inference, suggesting that the paradigm remains relevant as visual pipelines evolve toward multimodal models and autonomous agents. To make their claims comparable, we evaluate all five families along shared axes of transferability, adaptability, and deployment readiness. Across the lifecycle, we find that most protections are still validated mainly against static or weakly adaptive adversaries, while evidence beyond controlled benchmarks remains scarce. We close by consolidating cross-stage countermeasures and open problems for robust, composable, and deployable owner-side protection.

</details>


### [134] [Trident : How to Break Deep Reinforcement Learning Cyber Defenses (Agentic)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04317)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Ian Bryant,Armita Kazeminajafabadi,Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,SungHeon Jeong,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Trident框架，首个将RLVR用于LLM红队攻击，在CybORG和CyberWheel上使DRL防御性能平均下降522%，并发现静态红队无法揭示的 emergent 攻击行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL自主防御系统仅用静态启发式红队评估，对自适应威胁的鲁棒性研究严重不足；RLVR虽提升LLM推理能力，但缺乏网络安全领域的基准环境和交互数据集，两个空白亟待填补。

Method: 构建Trident三组件：隔离沙箱动态基准（CybORG CAGE 4和CyberWheel）、含13,000+高保真红蓝交互轨迹的RLVR数据集、以及'Code-as-Policy'三元架构（Log Summarizer–Planner–Coder），将红队训练重构为上下文bandit，可训练Planner从日志生成攻击策略，冻结Coder转化为可执行Python策略。

Result: 单个可训练7B planner即让蓝队防御性能相对静态红队基线平均下降522%；自主发现诱饵规避、自适应状态优先级等静态启发式完全无法揭示的 emergent 行为。

Conclusion: 现有DRL防御存在根本脆弱性，Trident证明LLM驱动的自适应红队是揭示和评估防御系统真实鲁棒性的有效工具，为RLVR在网络安全的应用开辟新路径。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber defense systems based on Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) have attracted significant research attention, yet remain evaluated almost exclusively against static, heuristic red agents, leaving their robustness against adaptive threats critically understudied. Meanwhile, recent advances in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) have improved LLM reasoning, but their integration into cybersecurity remains elusive due to the absence of suitable benchmark environments and interaction datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce Trident, an agentic LLM red teaming framework comprising three components: a dynamic benchmark with isolated sandbox servers spanning CybORG CAGE 4 and CyberWheel, a dataset comprises over 13,000 high-fidelity red-blue interaction trajectories for RLVR, and a ``Code-as-Policy'' RLVR agentic architecture Trident Agentic). The latter reformulates red agent training as a contextual bandit via a tripartite Log Summarizer--Planner--Coder design, where a trainable Planner generates complete attack strategies from compressed execution logs, which a frozen Coder translates into executable Python policies deployed against live DRL defenders. Empirical evaluations reveal a fundamental brittleness in existing defenses: with a single trainable 7B planner, Trident reduces blue agent defensive performance by an average of 522% compared to static red agent baselines while autonomously discovering emergent behaviors such as decoy avoidance and adaptive state prioritization that static heuristics entirely fail to uncover.

</details>


### [135] [Combating Knowledge Corruption in Agent Systems: A Byzantine-Tolerant Secure Collaborative RAG Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04366)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Daqing He,Zijian Zhang,Ye Liu,Jiamou Liu,Zhirui Zeng,Zhan Qin,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Hongwei Yao,Jincheng An,Yong Liu,Yi Li,Qi Sun,Xiulei Liu,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SecureCollaRAG，一个拜占庭容错的协作RAG框架，通过动态GNN信誉评分验证文档来源，抵御知识投毒攻击，保持领域知识完整性，并在非独立同分布数据下验证鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统虽缓解幻觉，却引入知识投毒漏洞，攻击者可操纵文档污染LLM输出，现有防护不足。

Method: 构建多源知识验证机制，在代理系统中利用基于动态图神经网络的信誉评分验证文档来源，实现拜占庭容错与攻击防御。

Result: 大量评估与形式分析表明，在非独立同分布数据分布下，SecureCollaRAG对攻击保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SecureCollaRAG能有效防止隐性知识投毒攻击，同时兼顾领域知识完整性，为协作RAG提供安全解决方案。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation systems partially address the hallucination issues in large language models, it also introduces new vulnerabilities to knowledge corruption attacks. Adversaries exploit these vulnerabilities by poisoning documents provided by RAG system to manipulate LLM outputs. To counter this threat, we propose SecureCollaRAG, a Byzantine-tolerant collaborative RAG framework leveraging Multi-source Knowledge Validation Mechanism. Our approach enables agent system to securely verify document provenance through dynamic GNN-based credibility scoring, effectively preventing stealthy knowledge corruption attacks while preserving essential domain knowledge integrity. Through extensive evaluations and formal analysis, we demonstrate that SecureCollaRAG maintains robustness against attackers under non-IID data distributions.

</details>


### [136] [Large Language Models and Social Media Information Integrity: Opportunities, Challenges, and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04375)
*Junjie Xiong,Zhengyuan Jiang,Xiaoran Xu,Chi Zhang,Changjia Zhu,Ning Wang,Mingkui Wei,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Lingyao Li*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了2019至2024年间1048项研究，深入分析215篇代表性论文，考察了LLMs在社交媒体信息完整性中的双重角色——既增强虚假内容检测与防御能力，又带来生成高度逼真欺骗性内容的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的信息完整性面临虚假信息、谣言、社交机器人和隐私泄露等严峻挑战，LLMs的兴起既可能加剧这些问题，也可能提供新的解决方案，因此需系统性梳理其双面影响，明确现有研究缺口。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，从多数据库筛选2019至2024年相关文献，经过两阶段筛选（1048项初筛，215项深度分析），从技术能力、伦理影响和隐私维度分类评估LLMs在信息完整性方面的促进与风险作用。

Result: 研究发现LLMs能提升恶意内容检测和复杂防御机制，但同时增强误导性内容生成能力；存在跨语言检测、实时监控和隐私保护实现等关键缺口。

Conclusion: 当前研究仍需填补跨语言、实时响应及隐私友好方案等空白；未来应指引利益相关方在利用LLMs能力的同时，制定策略以降低其对社交媒体信息完整性的潜在危害。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools that impact information integrity on social media platforms. This comprehensive review examines the dual role of LLMs in both facilitating and mitigating various information integrity challenges, including misinformation, disinformation, fake news, social bots, and privacy concerns. \textcolor{black}{We conduct a comprehensive review of the literature from 2019 to 2024, screening 1048 studies and performing an in-depth analysis of 215 representative papers. This systematic approach allows us to identify key patterns in how LLMs influence the information security in social media ecosystems.} Through a systematic analysis of papers from multiple databases, our findings reveal that while LLMs can enhance detection capabilities for malicious content and enable sophisticated defense mechanisms, they simultaneously pose risks by enabling the generation of highly convincing, deceptive content. We categorize and analyze the potential and challenges across different dimensions of information integrity, examining technical capabilities, ethical implications, and privacy concerns. The study demonstrates critical gaps in current approaches, particularly in cross-lingual detection, real-time monitoring, and privacy-preserving implementations. We conclude by proposing future research directions and recommendations for stakeholders to leverage LLMs while mitigating risks in social media information integrity.

</details>


### [137] [DeepInvert: Semi-Supervised Embedding Inversion Against Obfuscated Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04477)
*Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Tao Wei*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出DeepInvert半监督嵌入反转攻击，证明现有混淆防御远不如预期安全，在ObfusLM上达到73.5% top-1恢复率，远超此前最好成绩26.2%，揭示效用与隐私间的根本矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云侧语言模型服务常处理敏感提示，混淆防御被当作轻量替代方案，但实际保护效果被高估，需严格检验其安全性。

Method: DeepInvert结合有监督影子数据训练与无监督一致性目标，通过混合训练流水线在标记与未标记混淆嵌入间交替优化，并针对不同混淆机制和架构（编码器/自回归）做防御自适应扩展。

Result: 在9种防御、5个任务、4种架构上多数场景优于先前攻击；对ObfusLM恢复率提升近三倍；发现任务相关权衡：保留实用性的混淆也保留可逆结构，抗反转的则效用崩溃，仅简单分类中部分DP防御可兼顾。

Conclusion: 呼吁重新评估混淆类防御，当前方案无法兼顾隐私与效用，需探索更稳健的保护机制。

Abstract: Cloud-based language model services routinely process prompts containing sensitive information. Obfuscation-based defenses---including ObfusLM, SentinelLMs, TextObfuscator, and DPNR---mitigate this risk by transforming prompt representations before transmission, offering a lightweight alternative to cryptographic solutions. We show these defenses provide far less protection than previously believed.
  We present DeepInvert, a semi-supervised embedding inversion attack that recovers original tokens from obfuscated representations with higher accuracy than prior methods. The key insight is that unlabeled obfuscated embeddings retain exploitable semantic structure despite perturbation. DeepInvert combines supervised training on labeled shadow data with a novel unsupervised consistency objective over unlabeled target embeddings, alternating between the two via a mixed training pipeline. Defense-aware adaptations further extend the attack to diverse obfuscation mechanisms across encoder-based and autoregressive architectures.
  Experiments on nine defenses, five tasks, and four model architectures show that DeepInvert outperforms prior attacks on most defenses. Against ObfusLM, DeepInvert achieves 73.5\% top-1 token recovery versus 26.2\% for the previous best. Our results reveal a task-dependent tension: obfuscation schemes preserving enough signal for utility also retain sufficient structure for inversion, while schemes resisting inversion collapse utility. On simpler classification tasks, some DP-based defenses can maintain both. We call for a re-evaluation of this defense class.

</details>


### [138] [Leak-Resistant Unlearning: A New Benchmark for Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Consistency and Recovery Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04519)
*Haoting Qian,Qingjie Zhang,Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Han Qiu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为\unlearning的新基准，用于评估大语言模型在多样推理路径和恢复攻击下的知识移除鲁棒性，实验发现现有方法均存在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要依赖单跳问题和少量多跳问题，忽略了知识非孤立性导致的多跳推理路径泄露风险，以及静态评估无法捕捉通过轻量级适应恢复已遗忘知识的脆弱性。

Method: 构建包含多跳推理路径和恢复攻击的基准，在3个模型、6种遗忘方法和2个精选数据集上系统测试，并分析遗忘质量、鲁棒性与模型效用之间的权衡。

Result: 实验表明所有现有方法对多跳推理路径和恢复攻击均表现出明显的脆弱性，知识可被部分恢复。

Conclusion: 需要更鲁棒的遗忘方法以应对多跳泄露和恢复攻击，同时需在遗忘效果、鲁棒性和模型性能之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Benchmarking machine unlearning methods is critical to understand whether sensitive knowledge is removed from large language models (LLMs) or not. Current unlearning benchmarks include mainly single-hop questions and a narrow set of multi-hop questions. Although effective, they still face two challenges. (1) Knowledge is not isolated, whereby diverse multi-hop reasoning paths can potentially induce knowledge leakage than normal queries. (2) Unlearning may be fragile: unlearned knowledge can be partially recovered through recovery attacks such as lightweight post-unlearning adaptation, making static evaluation insufficient. Therefore, in this paper, we introduce \unlearning as a novel benchmark to understand robust LLM knowledge removal across diverse reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We experiment with this benchmark on 3 models, 6 unlearning methods, and 2 carefully curated datasets. Results show that existing methods are vulnerable to multi-hop reasoning paths and recovery attacks. We further explore the trade-off among forget quality, robustness, and model utility for LLM unlearning.

</details>


### [139] [Checked-In Secret Detection: Strings Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04523)
*Zhengdong Huang,Kevin Li,Jinqiu Yang,Yepang Liu,Lili Wei*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出StringGroup上下文提取算法与Secretron检测工具，仅用33.2%的上下文保留80%语义信息，在SecretBench上F1达98.74%，并实际发现48个未知密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬编码密钥是严重安全漏洞，现有正则方法缺乏模式识别能力，上下文感知方法面临混淆鲁棒性差、跨语言泛化弱、上下文冗长噪声大三大挑战。

Method: 利用字符串作为代码语义关键信息源，设计StringGroup算法聚焦字符串字面量周围上下文，结合Transformer构建Secretron检测工具。

Result: 在SecretBench数据集上F1分数98.74%，混淆与跨语言场景下超越LLM基线，真实环境成功检测26个应用中48个未知密钥。

Conclusion: 基于字符串的上下文挖掘能显著提升密钥检测精度、鲁棒性与效率，且具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Hardcoded secrets in source code pose critical security vulnerabilities which can be easily exploited by malicious adversaries. Existing regex-based detection approaches suffer from fundamental limitations, as secrets often lack identifiable patterns, resulting in poor precision and recall. Recent studies have explored context-aware detection methods, as surrounding code can reveal the purpose of candidate strings. However, these methods confront three key challenges: (1) obfuscation robustness where models over-rely on easily obfuscated identifiers, (2) cross-language generalization difficulties due to uneven training data distribution, and (3) lengthy and noisy context that introduces excessive irrelevant tokens and slows inference. We observe that strings serve as a critical information source for code semantics, offering superior contextual density, obfuscation robustness, and language independence. Based on this insight, we propose StringGroup, a novel context extraction algorithm that mines strings surrounding potential secrets. By introducing a relatively simple modification to existing patterns that narrows the analysis specifically to string literals, the method achieves significant gains. With only 33.2% of the original context, it preserves over 80% of semantic information and significantly improves the signal-to-noise ratio for secret detection. We further design a context-aware secret detection tool, Secretron, based on StringGroup methods and Transformer model. Evaluation on the SecretBench dataset demonstrates high accuracy with 98.74% F1-score and strong robustness under obfuscation and cross-language scenarios, outperforming state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines. We deploy our tool in real-world environments and successfully detect 48 previously unknown secret keys from 26 applications, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [140] [Breadcrumbing Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04565)
*Xuebin Li,Hanqing Zhao,Siyuan Liang,Kejiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,Dacheng Tao,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM搜索代理在工具返回渠道上的脆弱性，提出了一种基于策略的长期攻击系统及Authority-Chain Hijack（ACH）方法，通过协调多个看似可信的操纵结果实现证据链劫持，显著提升攻击成功率，并引入Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution（TGSE）自动优化攻击策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理安全研究主要关注静态网页注入，但现代代理会跟进查询并交叉验证来源，单一注入页面易被稀释或拒绝。本文发现搜索和页面观察的传递渠道是脆弱的安全边界，通过受控的工具中介界面，在每次查询中仅附加一个受控结果即可反复引导代理的证据收集和最终答案形成。

Method: 提出策略驱动的长期攻击系统，引入Authority-Chain Hijack（ACH），将孤立的搜索结果和页面内容操纵转化为跨多个看似相互印证来源的连贯证据链；同时提出Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution（TGSE），从执行轨迹中自动改进攻击策略，替代人工设计。

Result: ACH在SafeSearch测试集上达到最高总体ASR，为55.9%的ASR和83.3%的MaxN ASR；TGSE最强单一设置在保留评估中达到71.4%的ASR和95.0%的MaxN ASR。

Conclusion: 搜索代理的工具返回渠道是脆弱的安全边界，必须重视跨轨迹协调的长期攻击；TGSE展示了从轨迹中自动演化攻击策略的可行性，为防御研究提供了新的对抗基准。

Abstract: LLM-based search agents are widely used for information-seeking tasks, but their reliance on external tool returns introduces a critical security risk: web content retrieved during execution is untrusted, exposing agents to prompt injection and goal hijacking. Prior work on search-agent safety primarily focuses on static web-content injection, but modern agents issue follow-up queries and cross-check competing sources, so a single injected page is often diluted or rejected. We show that the channel delivering search and page observations is a fragile security boundary: beyond exposing the agent to a single poisoned page, a mediated search interface can repeatedly steer how the agent gathers evidence and forms its final answer. Under a constrained tool-intermediary threat model, appending only one controlled result per query can substantially increase attack success when the evidence is coordinated across the agent's trajectory. We study this setting with a strategy-driven long-horizon attack system and introduce Authority-Chain Hijack (ACH), an expert-refined strategy that turns isolated search-result and page-content manipulations into a coherent evidence chain across seemingly corroborating sources. ACH achieves the highest Overall ASR among all baselines, reaching 55.9% / 83.3% ASR / MaxN ASR on the full SafeSearch test split. We further introduce Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution (TGSE), which automatically improves attacker strategies from execution traces, replacing manual redesign with trace-driven refinement; its strongest single setting reaches 71.4% / 95.0% in held-out evaluation.

</details>


### [141] [Adaptive Intrusion Detection System using Transformer-Based Neural Networks and Continual Learning Approach with Adversarial Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04602)
*Azizi Ariffin,Afif Haris,Faiz Zaki,Hazim Hanif,Nor Badrul Anuar*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应入侵检测框架，结合表格Transformer和类别平衡经验回放，在持续学习场景下实现高精度且低遗忘，但发现回放缓冲区易受投毒攻击，需严格保护其完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDS在流量分布变化后性能衰退，微调导致灾难性遗忘，从头训练成本高；回放方法假设良性流量单一且忽略缓冲区攻击面，缺乏现实压力测试。

Method: 采用表格Transformer编码器，配合类别平衡经验回放，每次更新重放良性流量稳定决策边界；设计类实例增量（CII）场景，并评估标签翻转和后门投毒攻击对缓冲区的影响。

Result: 在CICIDS2017上，传统类增量准确率0.9994，CII下0.9989，遗忘可忽略，远超微调(0.0052)、EWC(0.0324)、LwF(0.0699)和iCaRL(0.8770)；但1%标签翻转使准确率跌至0.0053，后门攻击保持0.97准确率且触发流成功率达95%。

Conclusion: 回放良性流量可恢复近联合训练性能，但缓冲区成为关键攻击面，其完整性是部署的严格前提。

Abstract: Network intrusion detection systems (IDS) trained on fixed traffic snapshots decay silently after deployment as threat distributions shift. Fine-tuning models on new attacks triggers catastrophic forgetting, while retraining from scratch is computationally infeasible. Replay-based continual learning counters this, but existing methods unrealistically confine benign traffic to a single early task and ignore the replay buffer as a potential attack surface. To address this, we present an adaptive IDS framework coupling a tabular transformer encoder with a class balanced experience replay buffer that replays benign traffic at every update to stabilize decision boundaries. We introduce the class-instance incremental (CII) scenario where benign flows reappear alongside new attacks as a more faithful stress test, and probe the buffer with overt label flipping and stealthy backdoor poisoning attacks. On the CICIDS2017 benchmark, our framework achieved 0.9994 accuracy under the traditional class incremental setup and 0.9989 under CII, with negligible forgetting, drastically outperforming sequential fine-tuning (0.0052), EWC (0.0324), LwF (0.0699), and iCaRL (0.8770) baselines. While injecting benign traffic into every experience proves essential for preventing forgetting, the replay buffer introduces critical vulnerabilities. Label-flipping collapses the model entirely (0.0053 accuracy at a 1% budget), and the backdoor maintains 0.97 overall accuracy while driving the attack success rate on trigger flows to 95%, evading standard monitoring. Ultimately, while a modest replay budget recovers near-joint-training performance, ensuring buffer integrity emerges as a strict operational requirement.

</details>


### [142] [Understanding Fault Tolerance of Adversarially Robust Pruned Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04173)
*Manali Dangarikar,Cory Merkel*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了剪枝、对抗训练和硬件故障对CNN鲁棒性的交互影响，发现对抗训练提升输入鲁棒性但增加硬件故障敏感性，而剪枝对故障敏感性影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在资源受限的神经形态硬件上面临剪枝压缩、对抗扰动和硬件权重故障三重挑战，但以往研究仅孤立考虑各因素，缺乏对它们交互作用的分析。
